            'CommandCenterFullController|m_OnClick|m_MethodName'

    Get-ChildItem .\Assets -Recurse -File -Filter "*.cs" |

    $EditorLog = Join-Path $env:LOCALAPPDATA "Unity\Editor\Editor.log"

    if (Test-Path $EditorLog) {
